### 2026-10-07 — Audit public map provider cost and data transfer

- Reviewed the current Mapbox lifecycle, resource index loading/query/cache paths, organization bootstrap, detail cache and marker bitmap loading against official Mapbox pricing and Supabase pricing/storage/API documentation. No application or provider configuration changed.

- Main scaling concern: the client eagerly downloads every 200-item cursor page on mount and every five minutes while visible; eight-row UI pagination does not bound network fetching. Production endpoint headers provide five-minute shared caching with stale-while-revalidate; detail and organization caches also exist. The paged database query selects full rows and requests an exact count on every page.

Static/lazy map initialization would be a product tradeoff; preserve the current immediate map-first experience unless requested. Documentation audit only; no tests or heavy graph regeneration needed.

### 2026-10-07 — Stabilize national nonprofit search planning

- Field publication stopped safely after search exceeded its existing two-second health budget. Saved progress: 20,045 organizations updated/read back and 401,498 categorized; exact field plan 005 remains resumable. No identity import or research restart.
- Live read-only reproduction: forcing a generic plan inside the real search RPC took 1,892.58 ms; forcing a custom plan took 16.173 ms. The equivalent generic prepared SELECT exceeded four seconds, versus 4.125 ms custom. Eight ordinary anonymous food probes currently passed at 83–480 ms; this intermittent recovery does not establish a permanent fix.
- Add function-local `plan_cache_mode=force_custom_plan` to the two directory search RPCs. No global database setting, timeout, grant, filtering, result-shape or index change. This avoids parameter-insensitive plans for text/state/cursor/category combinations. Small additional planning CPU per call is the tradeoff. See [PostgreSQL planning documentation](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/17/runtime-config-query.html#GUC-PLAN-CACHE-MODE).
- Disposable PostgreSQL regression passes: repeated anonymous queries under a forced-generic caller, state/EIN/text/category/cursor behavior, nested v2-to-v1 fallback, caller-setting restoration, suppression/curated exclusion and existing RLS/publication/rollback assertions. Hosted quality and required code-owner review remain release gates; migration not applied live.
- Rollout after merge: apply only `20261007190000_nonprofit_search_custom_plans.sql`, retain exact migration history, confirm both function settings and public search health, then resume permanent `filing-publication-20261007.mjs` from saved receipts. Category worker resumes after field completion. Rollback: reset only `plan_cache_mode` on both function signatures; do not reset other function configuration.
